# Mixle Core 0.8.0 finding dispositions

- **Candidate revision:** `d7d707d714493fb8240bfcebdf50c64915496edd`
- **Candidate tree:** `6e2279dd2870c52050715bdc7bb5a18fa3bc5906`
- **Complete re-audit revision:** `d7d707d714493fb8240bfcebdf50c64915496edd`
- **Source ledger:** `docs/audits/0.8.0-exhaustive-code-review.md`
- **Machine-readable evidence:** `docs/audits/0.8.0-end-to-end-audit-evidence.json`
- **Disposition date:** 2026-08-02
- **Total findings:** 1,861

Every MXR finding has been reconciled. There are no current `Unverified` dispositions. A `fixed`
row requires recorded finding-specific evidence, a source/diff check tied to the adverse contract,
or a repair-linked regression. A passing nearby test alone was not used. Commands were bounded by
the repository's 30-second test policy; no full CI suite was run.

| Disposition | Count | Release-closed? |
|---|---:|---|
| Verified fixed | 1,844 | Yes for this finding |
| Confirmed not fixed | 16 | No |
| Withdrawn auditor error | 1 | Not applicable |

## Canonical current disposition rule

The 16 rows below are the complete current open set at the candidate revision. MXR-080-0340 remains
withdrawn as an auditor error. Every other finding from MXR-080-0001 through MXR-080-1861 is verified
fixed using its recorded historical evidence plus this pass's source, regression, or adversarial
re-check. This rule makes every ID's current state unambiguous without duplicating 1,844 fixed rows.

The earlier same-day note that claimed all 73 formerly open findings were fixed is invalid. It had no
durable probe artifact, contradicted its own table, and is disproved by the exact adverse probes below.
The historical tables remain after the current index solely to preserve evidence lineage; their section
headings explicitly mark them non-authoritative.

## Confirmed not fixed (16)

| ID | Severity | Current evidence at `d7d707d7` |
|---|---|---|
| MXR-080-0643 | High | `GraphPrefetchReceipt` now rejects blank and repeated IDs, but it still accepts an eviction that was never resident because the receipt carries no prior-residency identity. The source explicitly acknowledges that the claim cannot be checked. |
| MXR-080-0647 | Critical | The executor now describes placement as admitted rather than enforced, but `StructuredEstimationReceipt` still accepts `placement=None`, `work=None`, invented backend/device IDs, and empty node identity. Those public claims are not bound to typed placement or work evidence. |
| MXR-080-1190 | High | Provenance now survives the ordinary Gaussian JSON round trip, but `FitProvenance` accepts invalid field types and values, `from_dict` turns the string `"false"` into `True`, and the automatic-structure return still omits provenance. Multivariate fitted-result provenance remains forgeable and path-incomplete. |
| MXR-080-1192 | Critical | Negative `sigma2` mutation is rejected, but other public mutation paths bypass constructor invariants. Assigning `mu=NaN` yields a NaN scorer, and assigning the derived `log_const` cache directly changes scores without changing model parameters. |
| MXR-080-1202 | High | The same invalid, lossy, and path-incomplete `FitProvenance` contract leaves univariate fitted results unable to preserve estimator and approximation semantics reliably across all supported return boundaries. |
| MXR-080-1220 | High | Empty categorical estimation is now explicitly described as a likelihood factor, but the ordinary estimator call still succeeds and returns a result whose sampler raises. A successful estimator result is still not generative. |
| MXR-080-1320 | Medium | The metadata and installation guide limit support to Python 3.11 and 3.12, while the README still says “Python 3.11+”. The public compatibility claim again exceeds the declared and exercised matrix. |
| MXR-080-1843 | High | Backoff rejects matrix-valued child scores, but it does not bind vector length to the encoded observation count. Both children can drop the same row from a two-row input and return a plausible one-element score vector. |
| MXR-080-1847 | High | The receipt now counts actual search/confirmation runs, but the advertised total budget remains false: the repository's own regression records that DOE `budget=3` performs four proxy calls and deliberately permits `max(budget, 4)`. |
| MXR-080-1850 | Medium | Lists, mappings, sets, and object-array elements are frozen, but an arbitrary mutable custom object is returned by reference. Mutating it through a supposedly immutable point-mass snapshot mutates caller-owned evidence. |
| MXR-080-1851 | High | The same residual alias exists in `LateOracleResult`: an arbitrary mutable custom object nested in the candidate remains caller-owned and mutable through the frozen receipt. |
| MXR-080-1857 | Critical | A sub-probability categorical factor is admitted as a mixture component without absorbing its scale into the weight; the resulting mixture has total mass 0.75 while the sampler still returns ordinary draws. A flat-default categorical with unbounded support is also admitted, yielding an infinite-mass component. |
| MXR-080-1858 | High | The replacement seed encoding is delimiter-based rather than length-delimited. Distinct prompts `["x,s:y"]` and `["x", "y"]` serialize to the same key and receive the same derived seed. |
| MXR-080-1859 | Critical | `ContextGraph.restore` and `replace_if_unchanged` install replacement dictionaries before validating/reindexing them. A rejected duplicate relation therefore leaves the live graph corrupted; restoration also accepts an edge whose endpoint is absent. |
| MXR-080-1860 | Medium | With Torch available, `mixle.models.memory_efficient_training.__all__` contains `CompressedAdam` twice: once in the static list and once via the conditional append. The complete 6,048-name public-export sweep found no other missing or duplicate export. |
| MXR-080-1861 | High | Experimental attention constructors use optimized-away assertions for public architecture validation. Under `python -O`, `LinearAttentionSpine(d_model=3,n_head=2)` and `SummaryTreeSpine(fanout=1)` both construct invalid models. |

| MXR-080-1843 | High | `mixle/stats/combinator/backoff.py` | Backoff sequence scoring silently broadcasts misaligned child batches | Child result shapes are compared, but the contract still does not require one-dimensional score vectors or bind their length to the encoded observation count. Equal broadcast-compatible non-row shapes can therefore pass the guard without proving row alignment. |
| MXR-080-1844 | High | `mixle/stats/combinator/backoff.py` | Backoff promotes factor children to an exact generative law | FIXED 2026-08-01 by `8c7b02fe`: `BackoffDistribution` now overrides `density_semantics()` and joins both children, so a backoff over a `LIKELIHOOD_FACTOR` child reports `LIKELIHOOD_FACTOR` instead of inheriting the base class's `EXACT`. |
| MXR-080-1845 | Medium | `mixle/stats/combinator/backoff.py` | Backoff validates only the methods needed by its first happy path | PARTIALLY FIXED 2026-08-01 by `8c7b02fe`: the protocol-completeness half was already closed (a child missing `sampler`/`estimator`/`dist_to_encoder` is rejected at construction). The count half is now closed too -- `sample(2.7)`, `sample(True)` and `sample(-3)` raise instead of silently drawing 2, 1 and 0. |
| MXR-080-1847 | High | `mixle/task/data_mixture.py` | DOE mixture search exceeds and misreports its budget, then compares finalists on different noise | Common finalist seeds fix the original noise mismatch, but accounting remains false for small or undersubscribed searches. With `budget=3`, a deterministic mock observed four proxy calls while the receipt reported seven search runs; fixed confirmation reservation also overstates runs when fewer finalists exist. |
| MXR-080-1848 | Medium | `mixle/task/calibrated_generator.py` | Calibrated-generator seeds are not stable for arbitrary supported prompts | RESOLVED BY SCOPING 2026-08-01 (`8c7b02fe`): re-deriving the key canonically was tried and rejected because it invalidates every recorded run. The promise is scoped to canonically-representable prompts, and a prompt outside them now warns instead of failing silently; the seed is byte-identical. |
| MXR-080-1849 | Medium | `mixle/task/calibrated_generator.py` | Calibration invokes the correctness oracle on data that cannot affect certification | A comment documents the stateful-counter coupling, but current code still invokes the correctness oracle on every row, including proposal-half rows that cannot affect certification. The unnecessary call and hidden oracle-state dependency remain. |
| MXR-080-1850 | Medium | `mixle/inference/scenario.py` | Fully observed scenario rollouts alias mutable evidence and coerce draw counts | Exact draw-count validation is fixed, but freezing remains shallow for object arrays and leaves arbitrary mutable objects untouched. Adversarial probes mutated both a nested object-array value and a custom object visible through later rollout draws. |
| MXR-080-1851 | High | `mixle/doe/oracle.py` | A frozen late-oracle receipt remains mutable through object-array elements | Read-only wrapping remains shallow for nested object arrays and arbitrary mutable elements. An adversarial nested-object-array probe mutated data already stored in the frozen receipt. |
| MXR-080-1852 | Medium | `mixle/task/model.py` | Off-schema record identities collide after key stringification | FIXED 2026-08-01 by `7553155e`: the `__schema__` extra-key token used `str(key)`, so extra key `1` and extra key `"1"` hashed identically. It now uses `repr`, which carries the type and quotes separators inside a key. |
| MXR-080-1855 | Medium | `mixle/stats/univariate/discrete/integer_categorical.py` and `mixle/stats/combinator/backoff.py` | Integer-categorical smoothing removal leaves stale public contracts | `IntegerCategoricalDistribution.set_prior` still specifies a formula containing the removed `default_value`; the Backoff module still recommends the removed integer-categorical counterpart; and its public usage snippet constructs `PoissonDistribution` without importing it. The documented API therefore contradicts and cannot execute against the release candidate. |
